The targets (top-1 85%, family 93%) are far above both the refit and the inter-judge ceiling, and the refit would move most labels for a 3-point gain, so **the arch16 model, calibration, seeds, star bias, generic floors, caps, fallback and why text are unchanged** (UI unchanged; churn, shares, stability and position conflicts as arch16). The new key and the sim population are there to fit against once the build list or the judging converges. **Spot check** (`spot17.script.test.ts`, `RUN_ARCHETYPE_SPOT17=1`, `ARCH_SPOT17_DUMP`): top 50 random, 30 simulated-league veterans with the play types the reading saw above their peers, top 75 real 2025, famous players.
- **Archetype feedback and tuning (archfb):** arch17 showed the judged answer key is itself inconsistent (two judges share a build for 55.5% of players), so the user's own picks become the answer key. **"Doesn't fit?"** (player page: an always-visible outlined button beside the archetype, 44 px tall on touch screens, and one in its popover; the popover opens below or above the label, never over the button (it used to open to the right and cover it); hovering the label previews the popover with a 400 ms grace period and the popover stays open while hovered, click/tap/Enter pins it until a click outside or Esc (fbfix)): a picker with 6-8 suggested builds (the reading's next-best candidates, up to four from his family first, then the best of the rest, named for his position), a search over every build his position allows (names and 2K aliases), "None of these / I'm not sure" and an optional "This is a much better kind of player for him" tick (the pick counts 1.5x). Names and descriptions only, no numbers (no-visible-ratings challenge). Saving shows a confirmation and the label changes at once: the pick is a per-player override (`overrides[pid]` in the league's feedback, `archetypeFor` returns it with `user: true`; displayed like any archetype), kept across preseasons until the user clears it ("Undo / clear my pick" in the same picker), and ignored while his position does not allow the build (0 position conflicts). Draft Scouting and roster cells only show the label (not added there: plain strings in sortable tables); prospects can be picked from their page. **Storage:** per league in the `archetypeFeedback` game attribute (`{ picks, overrides }`, exported and imported with the league file like `recordsBook`), and browser-wide in the meta DB (`archetypeFeedbackGlobal`, "Use my archetype picks in all leagues", default on): with it on, every pick is also saved there and the tuning is fitted from both. Each pick holds a snapshot (`tuning.ts FeedbackPick`): the shown and picked builds' terms as the model reads them (`snapshotRows`: every term of `model.ts TERMS`, the promised-class values, the family and the untuned part of the score) plus the reading inputs (ratings with sub-ratings, position, age, overall, prospect flag, box-score reading, play-type reading, traits, season, reading version), so retuning and the offline refit work after the player changes. **Automatic retuning** (`tuning.ts fitTuning`, in the worker, at league load, after each pick (debounced 250 ms), when the tuning panel opens and at every preseason before labels are stored): a regularised pairwise ranking fit (softplus of margin 0.1 minus picked-minus-shown score, 200 diagonal-Newton steps, deterministic, rounded) of per-build bias offsets (L2 1, capped +-0.5 z, then scaled by min(1, picks of that build / 3)) and, from 10 usable picks on, multipliers on the sub-rating, play-type and trait terms (L2 20, 0.7-1.3) and per-family offsets (L2 4, +-0.5). A re-pick of the same player replaces his earlier pick; "None of these" and picks of the shown build are kept but do not tune. 300 picks fit in a few ms. **Guardrails:** promise terms (absFloor, need, promFloor, ...), blocked positions, the generic fallback and stickiness are untouched (the tuning only adds to the score before classify's post-processing); `tuning.test.ts` checks at the caps' extreme: 0 position conflicts, no new label with a shortfall beyond the untuned one + 0.25, generic share +3 points at most, max share at most max(8%, twice the untuned). **Re-label:** the classify memo key holds the tuning's hash; live readings (prospects, players without an entry) use it at once, stored labels change at the next preseason (stickiness applies). With no feedback the model object is the shipped one (`applyTuning` returns it) and labels are identical. **Your archetype tuning** panel (League -> Archetypes): picks used, what changed in words ("More players read as Shot-Erasing Cleaner", "Fewer players read as rebounder builds", "Sub-ratings count more in the reading"), the cross-league switch, "Export my picks (JSON)" / "Import picks" and "Reset my tuning" (forgets the picks here and browser-wide; players the user picked keep their pick until cleared). **Developer path:** `RUN_ARCHETYPE_FIT=1 ARCH_PICKS=a.json,b.json ARCH_FIT_DRY=1 ARCH_FIT_OUT=/tmp/refit.txt npx vitest --run src/common/archetypes/fit.script.test.ts` runs the full arch14 refit (family classifier, ranker, 5-fold CV, ablations) with the users' picks as the answer key (`tuning.ts picksToJudged`: picked build as the judged build; 30% test split by a hash of the pick); without `ARCH_FIT_DRY` it writes `fitted.data.ts` (`ARCH_EXPANDED=1`: the expanded model; picks are read with the shipped reference norms). About 6 minutes for 500 picks. Tests: `src/common/archetypes/tuning.test.ts` (direction, bounds, no-feedback identity, determinism and speed, guardrails, file round trip) and `src/worker/core/player/archetypeFeedback.test.ts` (override and undo, prospects, storage in the league, reset, export/import, the cross-league switch).
- **Judging rulebook and rule answer key (archrules):** arch17 showed two judging passes share a build for only 55.5% of players, so the answer key is now judged by a written rulebook (user-approved): **`docs/archetype-rulebook.md`** (words) and `src/common/archetypes/rulebook.data.ts` (machine-readable). Every word of a build name (the concepts of `twoK/concepts.ts`: Sniper/3PT, Sharpshooter/Stretch/Pick & Pop, Rim Protector/Shot-Erasing, Lockdown, Ball-Hawking, Playmaker/Dime, Slasher, High-Flying, Post/Bully, Glass/Board/Cleaner, Lob Threat, Rim Runner, Mid-Range, 3-Level, 2-Way, Point Forward, ...) has the skill classes it promises and measurable bars: top X% on the blended class reading (as the head word and as a modifier, read through `ruleQuantiles.data.ts`), sub-rating leans with Sub-Ratings on, play-type shares with Advanced Tracking Stats (e.g. Sniper: three top 15% and range lean +0.84 z; Rim Protector head: rim protection top 20% and blocks lean +0.52 z; Lob Threat: finishing top 25%, max-vertical lean, roll-man or cutter share); Crafty/Skilled/Versatile/Riser promise nothing. A build qualifies when every bar is met, the head word's class is one of his three best skills and (generic or all-around builds) at least 4 of 10 skill classes are top 40%; precedence: specific before generic, coverage of his three best skills, family priority (head = his best skill), specificity, margin, id; none qualifying: the nearest specific build(s), never a generic. **Judge:** `rulebook.ts ruleJudge` (deterministic, 1-3 builds). **Rule key:** `ruleKey.data.ts` over the 549 judged players (`ruleKey.script.test.ts`, `RUN_ARCHETYPE_RULEKEY=1`; report `tools/archrules/rulekey-report.txt`): 487 qualify, 62 take the nearest build. Agreement (share a build / same first pick / same family of first pick): rule vs arch17 36.4% / 7.7% / 37.0% (test split 35.8% / 7.9% / 37.6%), rule vs arch14 45.3% / 6.5% / 41.5%, for reference arch17 vs arch14 60.5% / 17.3% / 52.3%. The rule key follows the reading's own class values, so it disagrees with judges who weighed real-life roles the ratings do not show (Daugherty reads as a handling, athletic big; Jordan 1992 as a perimeter stopper first). **Promises unified:** `score.ts promisesOf`/`promiseChecks`/`PROMISE_MET`/`PROMISE_ELITE` and the Sniper/High-Flying name checks read the rulebook (classes, promise floor 0 z, sniper floor 0.8 z), values unchanged, so labels are identical; `rulebook.test.ts` checks every vocabulary word has a rule, each build's promised classes equal the classes the rulebook requires, the elite promise is the rulebook's Sniper word, the judge is order-independent and never falls back to a generic build, and the key covers every judged player. **Shipped model on the rule key** (`fit.script.test.ts ARCH_KEY=rule`, report `tools/archrules/fit-rule-report.txt`, test n=165): top-1 30.9%, top-3 58.2%, family 69.7%; a full refit reaches 32.1% / 64.2% / 72.1% (tune 42.7%: overfit) with the match weight 1 -> 0.14 and need 1 -> 0.08, which would move most labels for about one point of top-1, so **the arch16 model, calibration, seeds, star bias, caps and fallback are unchanged** (UI unchanged). **archfb:** the rulebook is the reference for the developer refit; where a user has picked a build for a player, the pick overrides the rule-key label (`ARCH_PICKS` replaces the key with the picks).
- **The user's own 2K archetypes as the answer key (archuser2k):** the user pasted NBA 2K's archetype for about 700 players in three batches: current players (batches 1-2) and 2K's all-time teams of 14 franchises (Bulls and Hawks in batch 2, twelve more in batch 3). **Data** (`user2k.data.ts`): matched to `data/real-player-*` by name (diacritics, punctuation, Jr./II/III, a shortened first name and two nicknames ignored): 694 of 698 entries (unmatched: Khalifa Diop, Graham Ike, Jaden Akins, Carlton Carrington). Current players are read at the latest season (2027: the 2026-27 preseason, with the 2025-26 stats); an all-time entry at the best season by overall of his stint with that franchise (from the seasons the stats list him there; his best season overall where they do not), at half weight (`ALL_TIME_W`); a newer batch replaces an older entry of the same player, an all-time entry on the season of a current entry is dropped, two all-time entries on one season keep the higher 2K overall (Glen Rice and Mourning were split by stint), Trae Young's all-time Hawks entry is skipped. Split: 30% held out by a hash of the pick id (`tuning.ts picksToJudged`'s). **Mapping** (`twoK/user2k.script.test.ts`, `RUN_ARCHETYPE_USER2K=1`; 338 distinct 2K names): 23 are one of our builds' names, 5 a 2K alias and 7 a merged build's name whose promised skill classes our build keeps, 24 2K names we never adopted (17 the 2K matcher had folded into a build that does not promise what they do, such as 3 & D Two Guard into Gritty 3-Level Scorer; 7 merged builds' names whose kept build does not keep their promises) and 278 new (2K26-era names). **New words** (`twoK/concepts.ts`, `rulebook.data.ts`, `docs/archetype-rulebook.md`): Burner / Open-Lane, Hammer, Cutter / Off-Ball, Scooper, Pull-Up, Chaser, Grab & Go, Pressure Cooker, Wizard and High-Low have rules of their own (classes, bars, sub-rating and play-type leans, sub and play-type wants); Pesky / Pest / Bandit, Tip-In / Possession Extender, Bouncy / Athlete / Apex, Bruiser / Punisher, Facilitating / General / Dish, Hash-Sinking, Bomber, Tenacious / Warden, Paint Defender / Paint Protector / Terror / Shot-Axing / Fortress / Pillar, Break Leader, Splash, Downhill, Levitator, Slammer / Flusher, Dribble Tech / Off-the-Bounce, Floor Sweeper and Finesse / Dynamic joined existing words; no existing build's words, promises or wants changed (snapshot of every build and every 2K name). **New builds** (`twoK/user2kDefs.data.ts`, generated): a name with no build of its own becomes one with 3+ weighted players (`MIN_SUPPORT`; or 2+ and no build promising what it does) and a measurable promise; 40 did (33 new, 7 never adopted): Open-Lane Burner, Perimeter Shot Hunter, Shifty Sniper, Sharpshooter, Rim-Running Hammer, Rim-Rocking Stretch, Post-Scoring Stretch, Glass-Cleaning Stretch, 2-Way Stretch, 2-Way Mid-Paint Playmaker, Mid-Post Board Enforcer, PlayShot Point, Mid-Paint Pro, Mid-Paint Creator, 2-Way Iso Sniper, 2-Way Playmaking Creator, 2-Way 3-Level Dimer, 2-Way Mid-Paint Maestro, 2-Way Mid-Post Maestro, 3 & D Two Guard, Pressure Cooker, Point-Fading Wizard, Paint-Protecting Riser, Hash-Sinking Slasher, Middy Slasher, Mid-Paint Playmaker, High-Flying Sniper, Crafty Stretch, Stretch Four, Stretch Five, Skilled Stretch, Stretch Riser, Catch & Shoot Sniper, Bouncy Sharpshooter, Bucket Chaser, 2-Way Crafty Sniper, 2-Way High-Flying Slasher, Shot-Erasing Rim Wrecker, Shot-Erasing Stretch, Wing-Running High Flyer (242 builds in all). Each profile is made the way a 2K build's is (`twoK/build2k.script.test.ts`), from the user's players instead of converted 2K builds: the weighted centroid of their readings' shape relative to their own level (`prepare` z), the strongest six dimensions and two weakest, plus what the name's words promise (weights, floors, expanded-ratings refinements), the sub-rating leans they share as light extra wants (`sw`), positions from the 2K positions a quarter or more of them list (narrowed by the name's position word and the hard blocks), the height gate from their heights, the family from the head word. Their match tables come from the calibration's reference league (both rating sets); then the share trim, the star trim (new builds included) and a real-player trim (`ARCH_U2K_MODE=trim`: each new build at most 2.5% of the real players 2016-2027 and 3.5% of the top 150s, by a step on its bias). Two spot biases (`SPOT_BIAS`): Mid-Post Board Enforcer (Giannis, the arch16 user report) and Post-Scoring Stretch (Klay Thompson). **Aliases** (`twoK/user2kMap.data.ts`): the 258 other new names become a name of their closest build for League > Archetypes' search: the builds his 2K positions allow with the most promised classes in common, then the best fit for the name's players (Diming Post Popper -> Post-Dimming Board Enforcer, Possession Extender, Switch Defender, the Board Enforcer / Board Hunter / Paint Protector / Post Bully families, ...); a merged build's name stays with the build it was merged into; one name is left unmapped (Showmaker: a nickname that promises nothing measurable). Search finds every name; the label shown is the build's. `ARCHETYPE_READING_VERSION` 15. **Held-out result** (the test split, 30%, weighted n = 171.5; new builds and closest-build mapping from the tune split only, so the held-out players never shaped a profile): before -> after top-1 0.9% -> 1.2%, top-3 2.0% -> 4.4%, family 27.4% -> 30.3%; current players 0.7% -> 1.4%, 2.1% -> 4.9%, 27.3% -> 30.1% (n = 143); all-time teams 1.8% -> 0.0%, 1.8% -> 1.8%, 28.1% -> 31.6% (n = 28.5: the all-time players' real-data ratings fit 2K's picture of them poorly); names that became builds 0.0% -> 1.6% / 0.0% -> 10.4% / 34.4% -> 40.0%. 2K's labels are barely predictable from the ratings the game has (the shipped reading agreed with 2.3% of the whole list), so the gains are small. **Refit** (`fit.script.test.ts ARCH_PICKS=<the tune split as a pick file>`; new: `ARCH_FIT_PRIOR=shipped` regularises toward the shipped model, `ARCH_FIT_KEEP_FAM=1` keeps its family classifier, `ARCH_PICKS_ADD=1` keeps the judged set, `ARCH_FIT_FREEZE=terms` keeps terms at the prior; the picks carry the real player's key and weight, `tuning.ts refitPicks` keeps picks of the shown build too): picks only, top-1 / top-3 / family 3.2% / 7.3% / 34.7% but 262 of the 694 labels poor fits (39 before: the fit drops the name-promise terms, need 0.0, promise floor 0.1); with the judged set 2.6% / 8.5% / 32.9%, 221 poor fits; promise terms frozen 2.0% / 6.4% / 29.7% (family worse), 29 poor fits; the in-game archfb tuning fitted from the tune picks 2.0% / 5.5% / 33.5%. None is a clear gain with the guardrails kept, so **the shipped model is unchanged** (fitted.data.ts). **Guardrails** (expanded off, before -> after): churn 5.9% -> 4.9%; max share random league 2.6% -> 2.1%, real 2016-2025 6.1% -> 2.9%, prospects 5.9% -> 5.8%, real 2025 top 150 4.7% -> 6.0% (Mid-Paint Pro); generic share real 2025 8.7% -> 2.3% (top 150 6.0% -> 1.3%); poor fit league 4.1% -> 3.6%, veterans 7.5% -> 6.3%, prospects 4.4% -> 4.4%, real 5.6% -> 3.8%; +-3 / +-6 stability 84.0 / 70.6% -> 83.3 / 69.5%; position conflicts 0; name promises 98.9% -> 99.1% (185 of 186 builds with 10+ holders keep every promise for 90%+); gold listed 56.6% -> 48.7% (role 78.8% -> 78.8%: the gold lists predate the new builds). Seeds regenerated. **archfb guardrail in classify:** with a user tuning on (a model with `buildBias`) a tuned label he falls short of by more than 0.25 z beyond the untuned label's shortfall is not given (`tuning.test.ts` found one at the caps' extreme with the new builds); without a tuning nothing changes. **Export:** _Archetype tuning only_ (see Downloads). Tests: `archetypes.test.ts` (merged names, custom builds' aliases, five pins: LeBron Mid-Paint Playmaker, his 2K archetype exactly; Klay Skilled Stretch; Brook Lopez Shot-Erasing Stretch; Korver Crafty Stretch; Wembanyama Paint-Protecting Riser), `rulebook.test.ts` (every new word has a rule), `archetypeFeedback.test.ts` (the tuning-only file round trip).
- **arch2kfix (this round's gaps, 2K labels, a words-only why):** **Season cap and answer-key guard** (`twoK/user2k.script.test.ts`, `ARCH_U2K_MODE=fix`, after the trim; norms biases only, no build or profile changed): (1) no new build above 4.5% (`SEASON_TOP_CAP`, 6 of 150) of any one season's top 150 real players 2016-2027, read as the game reads them (classify, with stats): the trim's caps pooled the seasons, which let Mid-Paint Pro hold 6.0% of the 2025 top 150. A step is kept only if it adds no generic label and no poor fit among the top 150s and all 2025 players; Middy Slasher, Hash-Sinking Slasher and Mid-Paint Playmaker stopped there (a further step would add a generic label), so one season's top 150 keeps 5.3-6.0% of them, the 2025 top 150 at most 4.7%. Biases: Mid-Paint Pro -0.83 -> -1.16, Middy Slasher -0.27 -> -0.7, Hash-Sinking Slasher -0.26 -> -0.86, Mid-Paint Playmaker 0 -> -0.5, Bucket Chaser 0 -> -0.4, Mid-Paint Creator 0 -> -0.2. (2) The answer-key guard: a list player whose 2K label is one of our existing builds (or its 2K alias), read as a new build that beats it by at most 1 z (`GUARD_RANGE`), gets it back, first by raising the existing build (held to every population's cap: real players, each season's top 150, a generated league 2.5% and its draft prospects 4%), else by cutting the new build (kept only if none of that build's own list players lose it and it adds no generic label or poor fit). One player qualified: Kyrie Irving (2027, 2K Iso Savant) read Mid-Paint Creator, Iso Savant 0.62 behind; raising Iso Savant (-0.54 -> 0.31) made it 8.6% of draft prospects, so Mid-Paint Creator was cut (-0.2 -> -1.05; its six list players keep it) and he reads Iso Savant again (pinned in `archetypes.test.ts`). The other existing-label misses are 1.8 or more behind (Garland 2023, Crafty Offensive Engine) or not allowed at their position, and are left. `ARCHETYPE_READING_VERSION` 16; seeds regenerated. **Gold lists** (`gold.data.ts`): sixteen lists gained the archuser2k build the reading picks where the player's 2K label or his clear role supports it (LeBron 2025 Mid-Paint Playmaker, his 2K label; Klay 2025 Skilled Stretch; Brook Lopez 2025 and Ibaka 2017 Shot-Erasing Stretch; Korver 2021 Crafty Stretch; Wembanyama 2025, Duncan 2003 and Ewing 1990 Paint-Protecting Riser; Booker 2022 Hash-Sinking Slasher; Davis 2020, 2K Backline Paint Master, and David Robinson 1995 Shot-Erasing Rim Wrecker; Duncan Robinson 2020, 2K Sharpshooter, Stretch Riser; Jordan 1991, 2K 2-Way Mid-Post Maestro, 2-Way Mid-Paint Maestro; Pippen 1994 and Payton 1996 2-Way Mid-Paint Playmaker; Ginobili 2008 2-Way Playmaking Creator); Bertans 2018 as a Shot-Erasing Stretch was not added (no rim protection in his role). **Metrics** (expanded off, master -> after): gold listed 48.7% -> 49.6% with the old lists -> 63.7% with the new (role 78.8% -> 79.6%); churn 4.9% -> 5.4%; max share random league 2.1% -> 2.1%, real 2016-2025 2.9% -> 3.4% (Open-Lane Burner), prospects 5.8% -> 5.8%, real 2025 top 150 6.0% (Mid-Paint Pro and Hash-Sinking Slasher) -> 4.7% (Middy Slasher; Mid-Paint Pro 4.0%); generic share real 2025 2.3% -> 2.3%, top 150 1.3% -> 1.3%; poor fit league 3.6% -> 3.5%, veterans 6.3% -> 6.3%, prospects 4.4% -> 4.5% (one of 1,000), real 3.8% -> 3.8%, real 2025 4.6% -> 4.6% (top 150 5.3% -> 5.3%); +-3 / +-6 stability 83.3 / 69.5% -> 82.9 / 69.7%; position conflicts 0; name promises 99.1% -> 99.0% (186 of 188 builds with 10+ holders keep every promise for 90%+, was 185 of 186). The user's list (held-out 30%): top-1 5.0% -> 5.0%, top-3 12.0% -> 12.5%, family 35.6% -> 35.0%; 35 of 694 labels changed, poor fits among them 35 -> 30. **Westbrook / Korver position re-fit (sgdrift's offered option) not done:** it re-fits the improved positions so the pinned players keep their old positions (Westbrook 2025 G, Korver 2021 SF). Westbrook's listed position is PG and Korver's SF/SG, so it cannot raise the real-NBA position accuracy for them, and in sgdrift's measurements it made the position shares worse (share error 12.2 -> 28.1 against the real listed shares; a 2-league run still drifted PG 12 -> 14.5 and G 13 -> 9.6, against about 12.7 / 10.9 shipped); their labels at the current positions are in position (Mid-Range Slashing Cleaner is allowed at PG, Crafty Stretch at GF; position conflicts 0) and Korver's is now in his gold list. **Archetype Label Source** (League Extras -> Archetypes, `archetypeLabelSource`): Game archetypes (default: nothing changes), 2K labels when known, Show both. NBA 2K's own archetype is looked up from the user's list (`twoK/user2kLookup.ts` on `user2k.data.ts`, the data; nothing per player in the UI) by the player's real-player id (srID, the data's slug) and last name, in the matched season only: an all-time entry in the season archuser2k matched it to, a current entry in 2027 and 2026. Random and fictional players have no srID and never match. 2K labels when known: those players' label is 2K's everywhere a label is shown (player page, Draft Scouting, Advanced Player Search, Roster composition; the popover adds "NBA 2K's archetype for him. In this game: ..."). Show both: the player page adds a small "2K: <name>" line under the archetype, compact lists show "<label> (2K: <name>)", and League -> Archetypes shows it after a best player. League -> Archetypes' search finds a build by its holders' 2K labels whatever the setting (search only: not shown in the default). Tests: `twoK/user2kLookup.test.ts`, `player/archetype.test.ts`. **Why, in words** (user request, `whyText.ts`): the popover's why is a bulleted list again, 2-4 bullets, no numbers anywhere (no ratings, no percents), so nothing to hide under the no-visible-ratings challenge. Drivers as in arch16 (wanted sub-ratings, then play types, then visible traits, then base dimensions), one bullet each, worded by his standing against players of his position, read from the ratings the user sees (scouting fuzz as on the ratings pages): Elite (z 1.75+), Strong (1+), Above-average (0.4+), Relies on (0+, only when it leads the list); a sub adds 0.35 x its lean to its base dimension's standing; play types by how far above his position's share he runs them (Lives on / Runs a lot of / Plenty of); traits by name ("Glass Cleaner trait"); a dimension the build wants low as "Low ball handling" when he is 0.5 below his position. Never a strength below average, never two bullets on one skill (a sub and its base dimension), always at least one (else "Relies on" his best wanted dimension). The compact one-line form (`whyLine`) is the top two bullets joined with " · ". Examples (real 2025): Gobert "Shot Blocking 70 and Interior D 65 at his size" -> Elite shot blocking / Elite interior defense / Elite finishing / Low three-point shooting; Curry "Three-point shooting 83 and Free throw shooting 79 at his size" -> Elite three-point shooting / Strong free throw shooting / Elite scoring volume. Tests: `whyText.test.ts` (the top driver still leads, no digits for 120 real and 150 generated players, 1-4 bullets, no repeats, stable, the shown ratings set the words). **Sweep fixes:** `twoK/user2kDefs.data.ts`'s kinds type allows `merged` (tsc --build); the tuning panel's exports are BOM-free (`jsonFileBytes`) and the fit and user2k scripts strip a leading byte order mark from pick files.
- **archv2 (the v2 engine, the only one; archgaps builds and fixes):** every league, old and new, is labeled by v2 (`common/archetypes/v2/engine.ts classifyV2`; the worker's `classifyPlayer`, the fuzzed prospect label and the regenerator's preview). There is no engine setting (a short-lived opt-in `archetypeEngine` option was removed). `ARCHETYPE_READING_VERSION` 17: current labels stored by the earlier reading are read again at once (live) and stored at the next preseason; past seasons' labels stay. The earlier single-build reading (`classify.ts classify`, the fitted model, rulebook and builds) is kept only as one of v2's voters and as its presets: (1) compositional names like 2K's (`grammar.ts`): a core role (Playmaker/Dimer, Offensive Engine, Creator, Shot Hunter, Sniper, Sharpshooter, Stretch (bigs), Bucket Chaser, Mid-Range Maestro, Slasher, Finisher, Open-Lane Burner, High Flyer, Off-Ball Cutter (the rulebook `cutter` word), Rim Hammer, Rim Runner, Lob Threat, Lockdown Defender, Ball Hawk, Shot Eraser, Board Enforcer, Board Hunter, Post Bully, Post Pro, Glue Wing, Glue Guard, Steady Game Manager, Energy Big) and up to two descriptors (2-Way, Crafty, Physical, High-Flying, Ball-Hawking, Defensive, Shot-Erasing, Glass-Cleaning, Board-Hunting, Playmaking, Iso, 3-Level, Inside-Out, 3PT, Stretch, Mid-Paint, Mid-Post, Middy, Pull-Up, Post-Scoring, Slashing, Rim-Rocking, Rim-Running), in 2K's order (2-Way, style word, skill words, role), never two words of one kind (`compatible`); each word is a yes/no test against players at his position (`tests.ts`: the rulebook word's bars read through per-position percentile tables `peers.data.ts`, its sub-rating and play-type bars, the Sniper/High-Flying bars and the promise floor; custom tests for Crafty and the gap roles), so a name only says what he has. Adding a word is one table entry. A composed name that is a build's name or alias (including the user's 2K names) and whose promises he keeps is that build (id, description, colours, family); otherwise it is shown with a generated description under a composed id `v2:<role>:<descs>:<group>` that `archetypeById` resolves (why text, popover, League -> Archetypes and roster composition work on it). (2) Hierarchical: three family voters (the earlier reading's label's family; the k nearest labeled players; the rulebook's best-ranked role) with the fitted family classifier; two agreeing -> labeled normally in that family, all disagreeing -> the safest label of the most-voted family (role only, or the earlier reading's label) at half confidence. (3) kNN (`knn.ts`, `knn.data.ts`): the user's 2K list (all-time at 0.5) and the judged key's first builds, features = the family classifier's inputs standardized per position bucket, k = 15 distance-weighted; votes for family, role, descriptors, display words and the exact build; a labeled real player never votes for himself (by srID). (4) Hysteresis: last season's label stays unless beaten by 0.5 (was 0.25; churn 6.0% -> 4.6%); the earlier reading's two runners-up are candidates too (held-out 2K top-3 6.4% -> 7.6%). Script: `v2/v2.script.test.ts` (`RUN_ARCHETYPE_V2=1`, `ARCH_V2_MODE=data|tune|eval`). Tests: `v2/grammar.test.ts`. **Gap builds (user-approved, scratchpad archgaps):** Glue Wing (GF/SF/F), Glue Guard (PG/G/SG), Steady Game Manager (PG/G), Energy Big (PF/FC/C) as custom builds with ceilings on every other skill (no standout), rulebook words Glue / Game Manager / Energy (`rulebook.data.ts`, `concepts.ts`), match tables and a real-player trim (`twoK/user2k.script.test.ts ARCH_U2K_GAP=1`), and as v2 roles; Wing Warden and Perimeter Lock are 2K aliases of Glue Wing. Biases raised (Glue Wing +1.0, Glue Guard +0.4, Steady Game Manager +1.1, Energy Big +0.8) until the no-standout ceilings, not the bias, held them: random league / real 2016-2025 Glue Guard 2.0 / 0.85%, Glue Wing 0.57 / 0.64%, Energy Big 0.63 / 0.56%, Steady Game Manager 0.27 / 0.59% (analysis estimate about 2.5% for the Glue builds; few players pass the ceilings). **Fixes (user-approved):** A athletic names (High-Flying, Aerial, Glide, Athletic, Force of Nature, Burner, Open-Lane, Wing/Rim Runner, Speedy) need athleticism at least ordinary at his position (`score.ts ATH_NAME_RE`, `ATH_FLOOR_K` 2): athletic-named players below their position's median athleticism, random league 6.2% -> 4.4%, real 7.5% -> 5.1%; B Crafty Mid-Post Scorer open at PF/FC (hgt 48-66): PF/FC holders 0 -> 5 (league) / 12 (real), Durant 2025 now reads it (pin updated); Elton Brand 2006 and Aldridge 2015 still read High-Flying Faceup Big / Slashing Mid-Range Maestro; C a pure or mid-range shooter (three or mid 0.8 z+) falls back from a generic build to a shooter's build from up to 2.5 z (`classify.ts SHOOTER_CV`, `SHOOTER_FALLBACK_Z`): shooters (top 20%) on generic builds league 2.7% -> 1.6%, real 3.1% -> 1.0%; D Athletic Hustler is called Off-Ball Cutter at wing (`posNames.data.ts`, the rulebook cutter word). **Before (the earlier reading) -> after (v2), held out:** user 2K all top-1 / top-3 / family 5.5 / 12.0 / 36.2% -> 5.5 / 7.6 / 35.3% (current 5.6 / 12.6 / 33.6 -> 5.6 / 7.7 / 33.6; all-time 5.3 / 8.8 / 49.1 -> 5.3 / 7.0 / 43.9); judged key 18.2 / 31.5 / 72.1 -> 17.6 / 29.7 / 73.3%; churn 4.8 -> 4.6%; +-3 / +-6 stability 83.2 / 69.2 -> 80.0 / 66.3%; max share league 2.1 -> 2.3%, prospects 5.8 -> 6.0%, real 3.3 -> 3.5%, 2025 top 150 4.7 -> 5.3%; poor fit league 3.1 -> 2.9%, real 3.8 -> 3.5%; position conflicts 0; promises kept 98.4-100%; gold 58.4% listed / 76.1% role (v2); family voters disagree for about 45% (the safe label). v2 does not beat the earlier reading on held-out top-3; the user chose to ship it as the only engine.
- **archv3 (v2 voters, the user's picks, speed, Glue, gold, composed names, batch 4, new names; reading 18):** (1) **Voters.** The neighbours' table was read wrong: `knn.ts N_FEATS` was a fixed 35 after arch15 added two play types, so the shipped rows of 37 were read 35 at a time and every row after the first was shifted (the kNN family voter was right 26% of the time on the tune split, close to chance); it is now `FAM_FEATS.length - 2` and the table regenerated (kNN 47%). Measured per position bucket on the tune split (2K list and judged key, the neighbours leave-one-out; `v2.script.test.ts ARCH_V2_MODE=voters`), family accuracy classic / kNN / rulebook / classifier: PG 43 / 58 / 42 / 57%, SG 44 / 50 / 31 / 44%, W 59 / 48 / 34 / 49%, F 49 / 37 / 44 / 33%, C 44 / 47 / 28 / 36%: the rulebook's role is the usual outlier (SG, W, C), the kNN and the classifier at F (before the fix the kNN at every position, 20-31%). Each voter's family now counts by its measured reliability at his position for the family it says (`v2/voters.data.ts`, smoothed; `ARCH_V2_WRITE_REL=1` regenerates it), plus a little of the neighbours' and the classifier's shares; the safe-label fallback is gone: the label is the best candidate overall, a candidate outside the voted family paying 0.5 (`famOut`). Builds the neighbours vote for (10%+) are candidates too; the runners-up put builds before composed names and the earlier reading's top builds first (`altBuild`, `altClassic`). Churn: last season's family keeps a head start of 1 (`famHyst`) and hysteresis 0.8 (a build he still passes is scored even when it is no longer a candidate). (2) **The user's picks** (`v2/picks.ts`): every pick with a build (this league's `archetypeFeedback` game attribute and the browser-wide picks, and the eight shipped picks of the user's 2058 tuning export, `v2/userPicks.data.ts`) is a labeled player from its snapshot (ratings, sub-ratings, play types, stats, traits) at weight 1.5 (2K list 1); a league player's picks never vote for him (`pickKey` by league and player id). The tuning's build biases count in v2 too (x5, `tuneW`), a build they favour is a candidate and pays no family toll. (3) **Speed**: one `scoreAll` per reading (the classic voter reuses it: `ClassifyOptions.scored`), the shipped neighbours as one flat index built once (`shippedIndex`), the warm-up after the tuning refit (its results were keyed on the tuning before the refit and read again on the first visit) and the engine's tables built at the warm-up: per player 6.5 -> 4.2 ms warm (8.1 -> 4.7 cold), Draft Scouting first load with 490 prospects (node, `draftScouting.timing.test.ts` harness) 3.3-3.6 s -> 2.2 s, repeat 0.1 s. (4) **Glue**: the no-standout cap is every class but the head skill under the top 10% (was every class under the top 20%, the head included), a role player not carrying the offense (usage under +0.5 z when he has minutes), the role names take no descriptor, and the roles a head start (Glue Wing 2, Glue Guard 1.4): random league / real Glue Wing 0.4 -> 2.4 / 1.4%, Glue Guard 1.1 -> 2.7 / 1.4% (max share 2.7%, the old cap); Energy Big no standout but finishing and athleticism. An athletic name on a player below his position's median athleticism pays 1 (`athPen`): of athletic-named players, random league 4.7 -> 0.6%, real 6.6 -> 2.3% (before -> after the penalty). (5) **Gold set**: v2's misses reviewed; twenty lists gained v2's clearly right label (`gold.data.ts`); hit rate 58.4% (master) -> 69.9% listed, role 76.1 -> 78.8% (earlier reading 63.7%). (6) **Composed names**: no empty part (`v2:<role>+<desc>...:<group>`; the reading-17 form is still read: the user's `v2:stretch::big` reads "Stretch Big"), a role alone is a whole name (Stretch Big, Shot Creator), the whole role after 2-Way alone (2-Way Post Bully), no 2-Way on a defender's role, no scorer's words on a Lockdown Defender or Ball Hawk, no three on a Maestro or Finisher, no Rim-Rocking / Wing-Running Maestro, Stretch only on bigs, Rim-Running for bigs and Wing-Running for guards and wings; a composed name only where no preset says him (no build within 0.1, the earlier reading under 0.8 confidence): composed random league / real 2.9 / 2.1% -> 1.5 / 1.7% (`grammar.test.ts` checks every composable name). (7) **Batch 4**: the user's Celtics (17, Tatum among them) in `user2k.data.ts` (2027, matched by name), `Rugged Rim Protector` in the name map. **New names** (shares random league / real 2016-2025): Switch Defender, a v2 role (perimeter and rim D both top 40%, athleticism top 60%, no offensive standout; 1.1 / 0.9%: Kyle Anderson 2016, Leaky Black 2024); 2K's own names composed as themselves (`OWN_COMPOSED_NAMES`) with the new words Rugged, Pesky and Rim Protector: Rugged Rim Protector (0.0 / 0.2%: Biyombo 2016, Claxton 2024, Favors 2022), 2-Way Middy Playmaker (0.3 / 0.2%: J.J. Barea, Calderon), Pesky Playmaking Creator, Wing-Running Sharpshooter and 2-Way Bucket Chaser (composable, rare: none in these populations); Defensive Playmaker was a build already (0.5 / 0.1%), 2-Way Sniping Layup Bully stays an alias (2-Way Inside-Out Creator). "Pesky" now reads steals (it was no "Pest"). The archgaps gap dump on v2 (`v2/gapsV2.script.test.ts`, scratchpad archv3/gaps): weakly read players 20.5% -> 17.3%; the remaining clusters (small floater guards, slow cerebral bigs, young athletic handlers) are covered by existing names. **Before -> after, held out vs the user's 2K list** (all; current; all-time): top-1 / top-3 / family 5.5 / 7.6 / 35.3% -> 5.4 / 13.0 / 40.8% (n 171.5 -> 176.5 with batch 4; on the earlier set 5.0 / 13.4 / 39.9%); current 5.6 / 7.7 / 33.6 -> 6.1 / 13.5 / 39.2%; all-time 5.3 / 7.0 / 43.9 -> 1.8 / 10.5 / 49.1%; judged key 17.6 / 29.7 / 73.3 -> 16.4 / 35.2 / 75.2%; churn 4.6 -> 3.1%; families disagree 45 -> 18%; stability +-3 / +-6 80.0 / 66.3 -> 78.2 / 62.6%; max share league 2.3 -> 2.7%, prospects 6.0 -> 5.6%, real 3.5 -> 2.8%; generic 0.4 -> 0.5% / 1.2 -> 1.3%; poor fit league 2.9 -> 3.0%, real 3.5 -> 3.3%; position conflicts 0; promises kept 99.0-100%. The user's eight picks, read with his tuning and the other picks (himself left out): Jokic 2027 Triple-Double Threat, Ledell Jones Crafty Mid-Post Scorer, Jahiem Brown 2-Way Point Forward Cleaner, Don Willis Backline Four (Shot-Erasing Lockdown at C), Trevelyan Davies Bouncy Sharpshooter; Pritchard reads Mid-Paint Board Enforcer (his pick has no tuning row: the shown label was composed), Brice Rufus Bulldozer (Davies' pick tunes Wing-Running High Flyer down), Justin Nichols Crafty Offensive Engine (2-Way 3PT Playmaker is a runner-up). Scripts: `v2/v2.script.test.ts` modes voters, picks (`ARCH_V2_PICKS`), churn, shares, who, composed; `v2/speed.script.test.ts` (`RUN_ARCHETYPE_SPEED=1`).
- **archcmp (other architectures against v2; none shipped):** v2 (reading 18) was compared offline with four other designs on archv3's held-out split (the user's 2K list and the judged key, 30% by a hash; the user's eight shipped picks leave-one-out), every model fitted on the tune split only and its weight in v2 tuned out of fold: (B) a 2K-style category layer (Finishing, Shooting, Playmaking, Defense, Rebounding, Physicals: weighted means of class values, ratings and box-score readings, the weights per position group fitted to the user's 2K overalls by positive least squares, R² 0.89-0.90; the pastes carry no 2K numbers besides the overall) with logistic family and core-role voters on it; (C) a depth-4 decision tree from the top two categories, height and position to the core role, v2's descriptor tests unchanged; (D) gradient-boosted trees (80 × depth 2) over the neighbours' features as an extra family voter; and their combinations, each with and without a label-noise pass (the 10% of training labels that agree least with their neighbours and an out-of-fold classifier at weight 0.3, sources by measured agreement: all-time 2K entries ×0.8). Held out (2K list / judged key): A top-1 / top-3 / family 5.4 / 13.0 / 40.5% and 16.4 / 35.2 / 75.8%; the boosted trees add family (D+N 43.6 / 80.0%, CD+N 43.6 / 80.0%, BCD+N 46.5 / 81.8%; paired bootstrap +3.7 to +6.0, P 0.98-1.00), the category voters the core role (+2.2 to +3.5 points), but no variant clearly lifts top-3 (−0.2 to +1.3, P 0.40-0.92) and the B and C variants lose the user's picks (family 62.5% → 25-50%). CD+N (best tune objective among the noise-pass variants, ahead of A on held-out family, top-3 and name-parts F1 on both sets) was built into the engine to measure it, and broke guardrails A keeps: gold set 69.9 / 78.8% → 59.3 / 76.1% (listed / role fit), max share random league 2.7 → 3.2%, veterans 4.9 → 6.1%, real 2016-2025 2.8 → 3.4% (shooter-family builds), churn 3.1 → 5.5% (2.9% with a family head start of 2), stability ±3 78.2 → 76.9%, about +0.3 ms per player; the tree alone (C+N) and the boosted trees alone (D+N) break the same guardrails (gold 60.2 / 58.4% listed, max share 3.2 / 3.1%, churn 4.3 / 4.8%), and the noise pass alone is neutral on held-out family and costs gold (66.4%). So the engine is unchanged (reading 18). Kept: the harness (`v2/archcmp.script.test.ts`, `RUN_ARCHCMP=1`: every labeled player's features and v2's candidates with what made each one (`V2Candidate.kind`, `V2Info.probs` / `classicConf` / `classicTop` / `classicScore`); `ARCHCMP_MODE=uncertain`: the 20 players of a 450-player sample league v2 is least sure of, for the user to label), the offline fit and report (`tools/archetypes2k/archcmp/`: an exact re-ranker of v2's candidates, the noise pass, the variants, the paired bootstrap), and `ARCH_V2_PARAMS` in the metrics, speed and picks scripts.
- **archv4 (a boosted-trees voter at a reduced weight, six ideas, gap names; reading 19):** measured on archv3's held-out split (the user's 2K list and the judged key, 30% by a hash; the user's eight shipped picks leave-one-out) with the guardrails against v2 as shipped (A): gold listed 69.9% (or within 0.5), max share random league / veterans / real 2016-2025 at most 2.7 / 4.9 / 2.8%, churn at most 3.1%, stability ±3 at least 78%, the user's picks 5 of 8 (62.5%), position conflicts 0, promises kept 99%+, speed +0.3 ms or less. Scripts: `tools/archetypes2k/archcmp/gbdt.py` (the voter sweep and its table), `words.py`, `calib.py`, `v2eval.py` (v2's own labels in an archcmp dump: top-1 / top-3 / family / role / name-parts F1 and a paired bootstrap), `v2/archcmp.script.test.ts` (`ARCH_V2_PARAMS`, `ARCHCMP_WORDS=1`: each descriptor's test at a grid of bars). (1) **Boosted-trees family voter (archcmp D) alone: not shipped.** Fitted on the tune split plus the picks (80 × depth-2 trees per family over the neighbours' features, class values and position; no noise pass), its family counted by its out-of-fold reliability per position bucket like the other voters (`gV`) and/or its probability share (`gP`), shipped to the engine as a 42 KB flat table (10 numbers per tree) read without Python (TS matches sklearn on 368 of 370 held-out players). Offline it adds family (gP 2: 2K / judged family 40.5 → 45.0 / 75.8 → 79.4%, +4.1, P 1.00), but in the engine every weight breaks the guardrails (2K t1/t3/fam; judged t1/t3/fam; gold listed/role; churn; stability ±3; max share league/veterans/real; picks): A 5.4/13.0/40.8; 16.4/35.2/75.2; 69.9/78.8; 3.1; 78.2; 2.7/4.9/2.8; 5 — gP 0.1 5.4/13.0/40.8; 17.0/35.8/75.8; 68.1/78.8; 3.4; 77.9; 2.7/4.9/2.9; 5 — gP 0.25 5.4/13.0/40.8; 17.0/36.4/75.8; 68.1/78.8; 3.6; 77.8; 2.7/4.9/2.9; 5 — gP 0.5 5.9/13.0/41.4; 17.0/37.0/76.4; 65.5/77.9; 3.6; 78.0; 2.8/5.1/3.0; 5 — gP 1 5.9/13.0/42.2; 17.6/37.0/77.0; 63.7/77.9; 3.6; 78.0; 2.9/5.4/3.2; 5 — gV 0.5 (reliability only) 5.9/13.0/40.8; 16.4/36.4/76.4; 64.6/77.0; 3.8; 78.0; 2.9/5.4/3.1; 5 — with archcmp's 2K-style categories as six more tree features (B's core-role part, not a voter), gP 0.25: 5.4/13.0/40.8; 17.0/36.4/75.8; 68.1/78.8; 3.6; 77.6; 2.7/4.9/2.9; 5. Even at 0.1 it costs two gold players (Capela, Hakeem 1994) and the real share (shooter builds), so the engine has no such voter. (2) **Six ideas** (held out, v2's own labels; 2K t1/t3/fam/role/parts F1; judged the same; base 5.4/13.1/40.6/20.2/16.1; 16.4/35.8/75.5/33.3/26.6): **a. learned word bars, not shipped**: for each descriptor the bar (top X% at his position on its rulebook classes; Crafty: offensive feel z) whose yes/no best matches 2K's use of the word on the tune split, one bar per word (monotone): twoWay 0.4→0.3, Crafty 0.5→1.0 z, Physical 0.4→0.55, Pesky/Ball-Hawking 0.3→0.05, Defensive 0.3→0.1, Shot-Erasing 0.3→0.5, Glass-Cleaning 0.3→0.05, Board-Hunting 0.3→0.1, Playmaking 0.4→0.1, Mid-Post 0.4→0.2, Post-Scoring 0.4→0.05, Rim-Rocking 0.4→0.6, Rim-Running 0.4→0.1 (word-level held-out F1 rises for Pesky, Defensive, Glass-Cleaning, Mid-Post, Rim-Running, falls for 2-Way, Crafty, Pass); in the engine 5.4/12.5/41.2/20.2/15.8; 16.4/35.2/74.8/34.6/27.8 (t3 −0.6, P 0.10), within two grid steps 5.4/12.5/41.2/20.2/15.8; 17.0/36.5/75.5/34.0/27.4 (neutral): the 2K names do not get better. **b. career-consistent reading, not shipped**: his ratings read 15/25/40% from his last two seasons (2:1): churn 3.1 → 2.6 / 2.4 / -, but held-out 2K family −1.4 to −2.5 (15%: 5.4/12.2/39.2/19.9/15.8; 16.4/36.5/74.8/33.3/27.2; 25%: 5.4/12.5/38.9/18.8/15.1; 17.0/37.7/74.8/33.3/27.8; 40%: 5.4/12.5/38.1/18.2/14.1; 17.0/38.4/74.8/32.7/26.7): 2K labels the current season, and hysteresis already keeps churn at 3.1%. **c. stats by sample: already done** (`stats.ts statsWeight`: none under 100 minutes and for prospects, rising to 30% at 1,000 minutes, +5% more from 1,500 to 4,000 recency-weighted minutes over three seasons); tuning it gives nothing (stats 20% / 40%: 2K parts 14.3 / 15.2; extra 0 / 15%: fam −0.4 / +0.2, P 0.00 / 0.55), kept. **d. era normalization: already done**: the norms (each skill's z at his position) are rebuilt from the league's own players each season and phase (`getLeagueNorms`), stat references from its last season. A 16-season league (new-league defaults, seed 4; mean ovr of the dumped players 48.8 → 44.8, wings and guards 3-5 points lower on most ratings): season 2025 / 2041 with the league's norms max share 3.5 / 4.7%, poor fit 3.3 / 5.0%, families within 3 points of each other; with fixed reference norms 5.2 / 5.1%, and only 35 / 41% of labels agree with the league reading; re-basing the word tests' percentile tables on the league too changes 8-10% of labels and nothing else (max share 3.7 / 4.7%, poor fit 3.3 / 5.0%), so they stay. **e. close runner-up, shipped**: the runner-up whose score is within 0.15 of the label's (`alsoMargin`) is stored with the label (`StoredArchetype.also`) and shown as a small "Also:" line in the archetype popover only (no other place): 17% of held-out players get one; among them the label is the 2K/judged build 8.1% (family 56%) and the runner-up 6.5% (family 55%), so the line doubles what is right for them; at 0.3 half of all players would get one. **f. calibrated confidence, shipped**: the shown confidence (the margin to the runner-up) did not track accuracy (held out: shown 6% → family right 61%, shown 98% → 58%; calibration error 28.2 points, Brier 0.348); it is now the chance his label's family is right from how far his family's vote leads (`confCal.data.ts`, a logistic fit on the tune split, the vote before last season's head start): calibration error 6.0 points, Brier 0.243 (shown 37 / 45 / 55 / 63 / 77 / 85% → right 48 / 43 / 51 / 53 / 69 / 79%). Labels are unchanged by e and f. (3) **Gap names** (`gapsV2.script.test.ts` on the current engine, with this round's 16-season league at 2025 and 2041: weakly read players 18.6%; clusters: small guards with a perimeter-D and floater lean, slow high-IQ post bigs, young athletic handlers (mostly prospects), no-standout wings, tall athletic bigs read Switch Defender): **Crafty Floater Guard, shipped** (v2 role, guards; "Floater" joins the Scooper rulebook word): finishing top 50% at his position and among his three best skills, a floater lean +0.5 z (without Sub-Ratings offensive feel +0.5 z), athleticism below the top 30%, no taller than his position's average, a three below the top 20%; head start 2.5; shares random league 0.5%, real 2016-2025 0.7%, simulated league 0.7-0.8% (2025 / 2041), real 2026-27 0.4%, real draft classes 4.2%; Dennis Schröder 2027, Tyler Herro 2027, Tyrese Maxey 2027, D.J. Augustin 2022, Jalen Brunson 2021, Trey Burke 2023. **Raw Athletic Handler, shipped, for draft prospects only** (v2 role, guards and wings): athleticism top 30% and handling top 50% at his position, offensive feel below his position's average, a three below the top 50%, passing below the top 40%; head start 2.5; generated prospects 0.6%, simulated prospects 0.2%, no real draft-class player; the user's Brice Rufus 2054 reads it. Open to every player it was the whole of a churn rise (3.1 → 6.2%: players aging out of "raw"). **Cerebral Post Big, not shipped** (post top 50%, offensive feel +0.5 z, defensive feel +0.25 z, athleticism bottom 35%, passing below the top 20%): random league 0.5%, real 2016-2025 0.0% (Zach Randolph 2017), and it costs stability ±3 (78.0 → 77.8%) and held-out judged top-3. **High-Post Hub: not added**, already 2K aliases of builds (High-Post Facilitator: Crafty Offensive Engine; High-Post Playmaker: Offensive Engine). **After (reading 19), in the engine:** 2K held-out 5.4 / 13.0 / 40.8% (same), judged 16.4 / 34.5 / 75.8% (top-3 −0.7: one prospect), gold 69.9 / 78.8%, churn 3.1%, stability ±3 / ±6 78.0 / 62.5%, max share random league 2.7%, veterans 4.9%, prospects 5.5%, real 2.8%, 2025 top 150 4.7%, position conflicts 0, promises kept 99.0-100%, classifyV2 warm 3.6 ms per player (master 3.6-3.7 on the same machine). The user's eight picks: Jokic Triple-Double Threat, Ledell Jones Crafty Mid-Post Scorer, Jahiem Brown 2-Way Point Forward Cleaner, Don Willis Backline Four (Shot-Erasing Lockdown at C), Trevelyan Davies Bouncy Sharpshooter (5 of 8 as picked); Pritchard Mid-Paint Board Enforcer, Brice Rufus Raw Athletic Handler (was Bulldozer; picked Wing-Running High Flyer), Justin Nichols Crafty Offensive Engine. Also: `longRun.script.test.ts` reads a contract's amount for the season (`getNearestContractAmount`; `Contract` has `amounts`, not `amount`), which broke `tsc -b`.
- **archv5 (the user's labels of the archcmp sample: absolute floors, the low-ovr label, his names; reading 20):** (1) **Ingest.** The user labeled the 20 players of the archcmp sample league v2 was least sure of (scratchpad archcmp/uncertain.txt, user-labels-uncertain.txt; `v2/archcmp.script.test.ts ARCHCMP_MODE=uncertain ARCHCMP_PLAYERS=118,20,...` regenerates the sample and writes their snapshots, ratings and sub-ratings identical). They are shipped picks (`v2/userPicks.data.ts`, uid `archcmp-<n>-v5`, with `label` his words and `why` his reason) at the picks' weight 1.5 (`knn.ts SOURCE_W`), the same path as his eight in-game picks. Mapping: 3-Level Scorer, Slashing Playmaker, Playmaking Shot Creator are those builds; 3 & D Wing is the 3 & D Specialist (now its name at wing, `posNames.data.ts`, as 3 & D Guard at guard); Perimeter Lockdown is the Lockdown Defender (a new alias, `engine.ts USER_LABEL_ALIASES`); the rest are composed: Glass-Cleaning Prospect `v2:prospect+glass:big`, Prospect `v2:prospect:wing`, Paint Beast `v2:paintBeast:wing`, Diming Paint Beast `v2:paintBeast+playmaking:big`, Interior Force `v2:interiorForce:big`, Offensive-Minded Guard `v2:offGuard:guard`, 2-Way Shot-Creating Slasher `v2:slasher+twoWay+iso:guard`, 2-Way Slashing Playmaker `v2:playmaker+twoWay+slashing:guard`, 2-Way Interior Finisher `v2:finisher+twoWay:big`, Athletic Finisher `v2:finisher+highFlying:wing`, 2-Way Athletic Finisher `v2:finisher+twoWay+highFlying:guard`, 2-Way Slashing Wing `v2:slasher+twoWay:wing`, Sharpshooting Slasher `v2:slasher+sharpshooting:guard`, High-Flying Shot Creator `v2:creator+highFlying:guard`, Post Playmaker `v2:playmaker+postScoring:big` (`grammar.test.ts` checks each reads back as his name and its role). (2) **Absolute floors** (`v2/tests.ts ABS_FLOORS`, `floorMiss`): a name whose words need a three has at least this 3PT rating, whatever his standing at his position: Stretch / Popper / Pick & Pop 45 (the user: 41 "way too low"; 29 of the 2K list's 85 Stretch bigs are below it), Sniper / Sharpshooter / Sharp / Laser / Automatic / Logo / Big Range / Silk 55 (19 of 86 below), 3 & D 53 (the user: 52 too low, 54 a 3 & D Wing; 2 of 7), 3PT / Catch & Shoot / Off-Screen / Splash / Shooter / Gunner / Bomber 50 (4 of 17). A name below a floor pays `floorPen` 3, whichever way it came (composed, the earlier reading's label, a build), so a defender's or another word wins when the shot is not there (the user's Perimeter Lockdown over 3 & D: Player 94 now reads Pressure Cooker, a perimeter defender, not 3 & D Specialist). Labels the floors change: random league 1.5%, veterans 0.9%, real 2016-2025 2.2%, real stars (ovr 60+) 0.3% (Vucevic 2023 and 2025, Crafty Stretch at 41-43 3PT, now Mid-Paint / Mid-Post Board Enforcer), 2025 top 150 0.7% (Vucevic). (3) **Low-ovr label** (`tests.ts prospectRole`, `depthPieceRole`; v2 roles Prospect and Depth Piece, family allAround alone, a rebounder's family with Glass-Cleaning): no skill good in absolute terms: every skill rating (inside, dunk, free throw, mid-range, three, the two IQs, handle, passing, rebounding; not size or the physical ratings) at most 50, or, 21 or younger, one real tool (60+) with every other skill at most 52, which his name then says (`engine.ts TOOL_DESC`: rebounding Glass-Cleaning, dunk Rim-Rocking, inside Post-Scoring, three 3PT / Stretch, mid-range Middy, handle Iso, passing Playmaking, defensive IQ Defensive, offensive IQ Crafty; only where its class keeps the name's promise). Prospect up to 27 (the user called a 27-year-old Prospect), Depth Piece after; never a draft prospect (every one would be: their label says what kind of prospect); head start `lowOvr` 3 and no preset in its place. "No class in his position's top 40%" was measured as the alternative and not used: the class reading is partly relative to his own level, so even a 40-ovr player has classes in his position's top 10% (Player 118's handling is in the FC top 5%), and a standing-only test (z at his position) failed both of the user's Prospects. Shares: random league 3.1% (Prospect 1.0, Depth Piece 2.1), veterans 0.4%, draft prospects 0, real 2016-2025 6.1% (Prospect 4.7, Depth Piece 1.5: real fringe players, e.g. Jordan Adams 2016, Ike Anigbogu 2018, Joel Anthony 2016), real ovr 60+ and 2025 top 150 0. A cap of 52 for all ages gave 6.4 / 12.8%, 51 4.8 / 10.5%. (4) **New roles with absolute tests** (`tests.ts`; their words in `twoK/concepts.ts` and `rulebook.data.ts` promise no class): Paint Beast (wings and bigs: dunk or inside 55+ and among his two best skills, 10+ over any shot, a three at most 35 and a mid-range at most 45, height 50+ or strength 60+, finishing top 50% at his position, not an anchor: his blocking and rebounding standing below +1 z at his position, so Gobert, Mutombo, Howard keep their names; one descriptor at most: Diming Paint Beast; head start 0.75), Interior Force (height, strength and rebounding 55+, three and mid-range at most 45, no finishing tool, not an anchor; 1.5), Offensive-Minded Guard (mid-range, three or handle 60+, defensive IQ at most 35, perimeter D below his position's top 70%; 1). Last season's role keeps its absolute bars 3 points looser (`absBand`, floors too). Shares random league / veterans / real 2016-2025: Paint Beast (with its words) 1.0 / 0.4 / 1.1% (Steven Adams 2016-17, Marvin Bagley 2019), Interior Force 0.8 / 0.2 / 0.7% (Asik 2016, Biyombo 2018, Bam Adebayo 2019), Offensive-Minded Guard 0.2 / 0 / 1.0% (D.J. Augustin 2020, Malik Beasley 2022; stars too: Booker 2020-21, LaVine 2022, Lillard 2024, Reaves 2025, at 25-35 defensive IQ). (5) **His wording** (`grammar.ts`): the finisher role at big is Interior Finisher; 2K-order combinations named as he says them (`COMBO_NAMES`, now with an optional size group): Athletic Finisher and 2-Way Athletic Finisher (finisher with High-Flying), Shot-Creating Slasher and 2-Way Shot-Creating Slasher (slasher with Iso), 2-Way Slashing Wing (slasher with 2-Way at wing), Post Playmaker, High-Flying Shot Creator (creator with High-Flying; at F it is the build of that name), Diming Paint Beast; a new descriptor Sharpshooting (guards and wings, Sharpshooter's bar and floor; "Sharpshooting Slasher"); 2-Way Slashing Playmaker and 2-Way Interior Finisher compose as they are. These composed names are reachable and keep every word's test, but rare (0.0-0.2% in each population: their words' relative tests and the presets usually say him first). "Defense over shooting" comes from the floors (a defense word wins once the shooting word pays). (6) **Measured, not shipped:** a 2-Way floor (2K's 2-Way players have defensive IQ 37+ in 95% of cases, the user's 56+), and athletic floors (the user calls a 52.5 speed/jump player athletic). **Results.** The 20, leave-one-out (his own pick never votes for him; `v2.script.test.ts ARCH_V2_MODE=labels`): as labeled 4 of 20 (Glass-Cleaning Prospect, Prospect, Interior Force, Offensive-Minded Guard), family 10 of 20; master (reading 19) 0 of 20, family 4 of 20. Stretch Big (Players 118, 326), 3 & D Specialist (94) and Catch & Shoot Sniper (435) are gone, as he asked; the rest keep a preset his words do not beat (Paint Beast 363 reads Rim-Reaping High Flyer, 201 Post-Up Glass Cleaner; 266 2-Way Power Finisher, 441 2-Way Bulldozer, 208 Big Glide, 356 Catch & Shoot 3PT Sniper, 238 Athletic Hustler, 87 Lockdown Defender, 348 Ball-Hawking 3PT Slasher, 435 Glue Wing, 162 Mid-Range High Flyer, 312 Shifty Sniper, 20 Physical Mid-Range High Flyer, 406 3 & D Specialist, 2 Board-Hunting Bucket Getter). His eight in-game picks read as before (5 of 8: Jokic, Ledell Jones, Jahiem Brown, Don Willis, Trevelyan Davies). **Guardrails, master (reading 19) -> reading 20:** 2K held-out top-1 / top-3 / family 5.4 / 13.0 / 40.8 -> 5.4 / 13.0 / 39.7% (the floors: 2K calls Moritz Wagner, Isaiah Stewart, Zach Collins, Bogoljub Markovic Stretch at 41-43 3PT); judged 16.4 / 34.5 / 75.8 -> 14.5 / 34.5 / 76.4% (the 20 labels as neighbours: without them 16.4 / 34.5 / 75.8, e.g. a judged Slashing Lockdown now reads 2-Way Slashing Playmaker); gold listed / role 69.9 / 78.8 -> 70.8 / 79.6%; churn 3.1 -> 3.8% (prospects outgrowing the label; 10.3% before the one-tool rule needed a 60+ tool at 21 or younger); stability +-3 / +-6 78.0 / 62.5 -> 73.3 / 56.4% (a uniform shift of every rating crosses the absolute bars by design: low-ovr label 2.2 points, floors 1.0, the new roles 1.4); max share random league 2.7 -> 2.7%, veterans 4.9 -> 5.9% (3 & D Specialist: 3 & D Guard 5.0%, 3 & D Wing 0.9% as shown), draft prospects 5.5 -> 5.4%, real 2016-2025 2.8 -> 3.1% (Prospect; the next build 2.8%), 2025 top 150 4.7 -> 4.7%; generic 0.5 / 1.2 -> 0.4 / 1.4% plus the low-ovr label 3.1 / 6.1%; poor fit league 2.9 -> 2.8%, veterans 5.4 -> 5.5%, real 3.3 -> 2.8%; position conflicts 0; promises kept 98.9-100%; classifyV2 warm 3.66 ms per player (master 3.6-3.7). Scripts: `v2.script.test.ts` modes `labels` (the 20), `archv5` (new names' shares, low-ovr share, floor relabels among all and stars), `stab` (stability and churn alone), `ARCH_V2_HITS=file` (each held-out player's label and hit).
- **archramp (ramps for the cliffs, the review queue, the user's league as a test population, no Prospect / Depth Piece; reading 21):** the user asked for a more robust reading. **Test population** (`src/worker/core/player/archetypeLeague.script.test.ts`, `RUN_ARCHETYPE_LEAGUE=1 ARCH_LEAGUE_FILES=<dir or files> ARCH_LEAGUE_OUT=report.txt`; `ARCH_LEAGUE_VARIANTS` parameter sets, `ARCH_LEAGUE_WHY=1` what moved with each flipped label, `ARCH_LEAGUE_DUMP` every label): a players export of one of the user's leagues (689 players in 2058: 482 on teams, 146 free agents, 61 prospects; sub-ratings, expanded ratings, traits and tracking stats on) loaded into the worker cache and read as the game reads it (the league's own norms, stat and play-type references, traits, prospects as scouted): stability under a uniform shift of every rating by 3 / 6, under independent noise of up to 1 / 2 on each rating (three draws), and churn with each player's last five seasons replayed from his ratings history (last season's label carried; the league's current norms throughout). The files are the user's and are not in the repo. **What flipped labels** (noise of up to 2, 509 flips): the earlier reading's own label had moved in 60% (its top three builds were candidates at 1 / 0.8 / 0.7 and the fourth at nothing), the voted family in 32%; the median score gap the new label had to close was 0.42. The bars of the word tests were not the cause. **Shipped:** (1) `classicSoft` 1.5: the earlier reading's builds are candidates by how close each is to its best (bonus falling to nothing 1.5 z behind, up to five), not by rank; (2) `presetSoft` 0.1: that reading's label takes a composed name's place over a band of its confidence around 0.8, not at the one value; (3) `hyst` 0.8 -> 1.2 (of the labels that changed between seasons and were still candidates, half trailed by under 1.1) and `keepWords`: a composed label that is kept is kept in last season's words (the neighbours' word for a role moved between seasons: "Rim-Wrecking Prospect" one year, "Rim-Rocking Prospect" the next); (4) `lowOvrRoles` off: no Prospect or Depth Piece label (the user: "I don't want Prospect and Depth Piece anymore"; 8.4% of his league had it), such a player is read like anyone else; the tests, the words and his own picks of those names stay in the data. **Built, measured and not shipped** (parameters, off): `rampLo` / `rampHi` / `rampPen`, the word tests' bars as ramps (`tests.ts`: every test also returns `soft`, its tightest bar in ramp widths, 3 rating points, 5 percentile points or 0.25 z, the promise floor, ages and the prospect flag staying yes/no; `engine.ts rampMu` the membership that scales a role's head start and what a near miss pays; the absolute 3PT floors the same way through `floorGap`): no gain in stability (user's league 77.2 -> 76.9%, generated 76.4 -> 76.5%) and three gold players lost (the no-standout roles, with their head starts, take the near misses); `famSoft` / `famOutW`, a voter torn between two families splitting its vote and the out-of-family toll rising with the family's lead: stability +0.9, churn 2.9 -> 2.4%, but held-out 2K family 39.7 -> 38.8% and gold role fit 79.6 -> 77.9%. **Before (reading 20) -> after.** User's league: stability +-3 / +-6 73.4 / 58.4 -> 77.2 / 64.7%, noise of 1 / 2 80.9 / 75.4 -> 82.2 / 76.5%, churn 12.5 -> 7.9%, 120 of 689 labels differ (67 of them the Prospect / Depth Piece players), 3.8 ms per player. Generated players (`v2.script.test.ts` eval): stability +-3 / +-6 73.3 / 56.4 -> 76.4 / 61.6%, churn 3.8 -> 2.9%; max share random league 2.7 -> 2.6%, veterans 5.9 -> 6.2%, prospects 5.4 -> 4.9%, real 2016-2025 3.1 (Prospect) -> 3.0%, 2025 top 150 4.7 -> 4.7%; generic 0.4 / 1.4 -> 0.6 / 2.4% (real 2025 top 150 1.3 -> 3.3%); poor fit league 2.8 -> 2.9%, real 2.8 -> 3.2%; position conflicts 0; promises kept 98.7-100%. Held out: the user's 2K list top-1 / top-3 / family 5.4 / 13.0 / 39.7 -> 4.8 / 11.9 / 39.7% (one and two players); judged key 14.5 / 34.5 / 76.4 -> 17.0 / 40.0 / 77.0% (classicSoft: +3 and +9 players; no Prospect: +1). Gold listed / role 70.8 / 79.6 -> 68.1 / 79.6% (three players; the gold lists were extended with v2's own labels in archv3, so they lean to the reading as it was). **Review queue** (League -> Archetypes, _Check labels_, `ArchetypeReviewQueue.tsx`; worker `api/archetypes.ts reviewQueue` / `reviewSubmit`): ten players at a time, players on teams before the next draft class and free agents, the ones the reading is least sure of first (stored confidence, then overall), one of each label and at most three of a family, each with his label, its description and the why bullets (words only); _Fits_, _Doesn't fit_ (then _Pick the one that does_ opens the "Doesn't fit?" picker), _Not sure_ (passed over for the visit), _Change_. A verdict is a `FeedbackPick` with `verdict` "fits" (picked = the shown build: a labeled player for the neighbours at the picks' weight, never voting for himself; no override, nothing fitted in the tuning) or "no" (picked null), stored with the picks (the league's `archetypeFeedback`, browser-wide when that is on, exported and imported with them, forgotten by _Reset my tuning_); a new verdict for the same player and season replaces the earlier one; a player whose label the user picked is not asked about. The card shows the fit rate (`tuning.ts verdictStats`: the latest verdict per player and season, overall and the weakest family with three or more), the number to watch when the reading changes. Tests: `v2/grammar.test.ts` (ramps in step with the yes/no they replace, no Prospect / Depth Piece), `archetypeFeedback.test.ts` (the queue, verdicts kept and counted, no label or tuning changed, replaced, exported, reset).
- **archreview (the user's reviews of 995 labels: absolute gates, honest names, ratings and traits only; reading 22):** the user reviewed the labels of three leagues and three draft classes (a strong league, 200 players; a weak one, 300 players, best ovr 55, median 46; a very weak one, 285, best 39, median 31; 210 draft prospects) and disagreed with 620 of 995, each with the skill the label names that the player lacks. Main finding: labels were picked from a player's best ratings, not from whether those ratings are good. **Test set** (`v2/review.script.test.ts`, `RUN_ARCHETYPE_REVIEW=1 ARCH_REVIEW_FILE=eval.json`; the joined review is built by the scratchpad's `archreview/build.py` from the user's visible-ratings exports and CSVs, not in the repo). (1) **Absolute gates** (`v2/tests.ts ABS_GATES`, `WORD_GATES`, `gateGap`): every word concept of a name needs the ratings the review judged it on, on the 0-100 scale the user sees, fitted to his verdicts (`fit.py`: each cutoff reproduces 85-100% of his verdicts on the labels that carry the word): three-point words 3PT 60; mid-range 60; post 55; passing 60; rebounding 60 ("Glass" in any form); Slasher speed 60 and handle 50; Aerial / High-Flying jumping 65, Riser 60, "Athletic" speed or jumping 65; strength 60 (also "Enforcer", "Hammer", "Force of Nature", "Beast"); perimeter-D words 55 with defensive IQ 40, "Lockdown" 55 / 50; steals 55 / 50; rim protection interior D 55 and height; 2-Way offensive IQ 50, defensive IQ 45, ovr 50; Triple-Double passing and rebounding 60, handle 55, ovr 55. A candidate below a gate pays `gatePen` 8 (the name he is shown as is checked: "Glass-Cleaning Playmaker" is shown as Triple-Double Threat). As a judge of the labels he was shown the gates agree with the user on 92.4% of the 995. In a real-players league (most players with a real-player id) each cutoff is read at the same standing among the league's players (`v2/gateScale.ts`, reference `gateScale.data.ts` from new random leagues): real players' ratings sit on another scale (Chris Paul 2025 passes at 58). A random league, however weak, keeps the absolute bars. (2) **Honest names** (`v2/honest.ts`, user design: "allow bad players to have decent sounding archetypes without unrealistic or elite ones"): after the reading picks, his tier on ovr decides the words his label may have: 60+ elite words (`ELITE_RE`: Sniper, Sharpshooter, Lockdown, Engine, Force of Nature, Prodigy, Triple-Double, Eraser, Maestro, Savant, Shot Creator, ...), 50-59 plain words only, under 50 an honest name unless two skills clear a real bar (`SKILL_BARS`, about 60: three, mid-range, post, dunk, passing, handle, rebounding, perimeter D, rim D, jumping, speed, strength). The reading's pick stays when it is honest for him; else the best candidate that is; else an honest name: **specialists** for one skill (Spot-Up Specialist, Corner Merchant, One-Trick Shooter, Bench Bomber, Shot-Only Wing; Defensive Specialist, Hound, Pest, Gnat; Shot-Blocking Specialist; Glass-Only Big, Board Specialist, Garbage Man; Big Body, Wall, Space Eater, Human Screen, Bruiser; Pogo Stick, Bounce-Only Wing, Track Star, All Legs, No Game; Dime Specialist, Pass-First, Pass-Only, Caretaker Guard; Ball-Handling, Mid-Range, Post-Up and Dunk Specialist), with **flaws** where his ratings show one (Earthbound Big, Slow-Motion Scorer, Empty-Calorie Scorer, Glove-Only Guard, Shooter Without a Jumper, Garbage-Time / Streaky Gunner); **developing** names with upside (22 or under, or a draft prospect, and potential 15+ above ovr: Developing Shooter / Playmaker / Defender / Rebounder / Scorer / Post Scorer / Ball Handler, Project Stretch Big, Developing Lockdown Wing, Stopper-in-Training, Rim Protector in Training, Raw Bouncy Guard / Wing / Big, Raw Athlete, Raw Big, Raw Shooter, Toolsy Guard / Wing / Big, All Tools, No Toolbox, Lottery Ticket, Late Bloomer, Project Guard / Wing / Center, Unfinished Product, Clay, Blueprint, Upside Play, Sleeper); **depth** names with no skill and no upside (Rotation Filler, Two-Way Contract, Ten-Day Guard / Wing, Practice Squad Big, Emergency Big, Journeyman 30+, Camp Body under 35 ovr, Hustle Guy / Junkyard Dog / Energy Big with the motor). A synonym is chosen by a stable seed (his league and player id), so a player keeps his name while his situation stays the same. Honest names are composed roles (`grammar.ts`, `v2:<key>:<group>:<key>=<synonym>`) with their own description and family; the why line names his best skills. No overall cutoff: an honest name is forced only under 50 ovr with at most one skill over the bar; from 50 it appears only when no label he lives up to remains. (3) **Ratings and traits only** (user request, `V2_PARAMS.useStats` false, `archetype.ts readOptionsFor`): his box score, shot zones and play types are no longer read, and the why line no longer cites them. (4) No Raw / Reserve fallback names (an intermediate step, replaced by the honest names). **Results.** The review (the labels the user was shown -> now): every word true (gates) 37.5% -> 98.0%; no elite word below ovr 60: strong league 86.5% -> 100%, draft classes 66-71% -> 100%, weak league 74.0% -> 99.7%, very weak league 74.0% -> 100%; honest names: strong league 2.0%, draft classes 69-77%, weak league 35.3%, very weak league 80.7%. The user's 2058 league (689 players with free agents and prospects): honest names by ovr: 60+ 0 of 95, 55-59 9%, 50-54 7%, 45-49 38%, 40-44 56%, under 40 80% (prospects 84%); 299 of 689 in all. Real 2025 players: 3 of the top 150 (Josh Hart Garbage Man, Toumani Camara Energy Big, Rui Hachimura Mid-Range Specialist), 361 of 701 (nearly all under 45 ovr). **Costs** (reading 21 -> 22): gold set listed / role 67.3 / 79.6% -> 39.8 / 65.5% (the gold lists name elite builds for players under ovr 60 and were made before the bars); the user's 2K list held-out top-1 / top-3 / family 4.8 / 11.9 / 39.7% -> 3.1 / 6.8 / 34.6%; judged key 17.0 / 40.0 / 77.0% -> 13.3 / 27.9 / 69.1%; stability under a uniform shift of every rating by 3: generated players 76.4% -> 63.3%, the user's league 72.9% -> 46.6% (absolute bars, tiers and skill counts are crossed by a shift); churn generated 2.9% -> 11.8%, the user's league 3.8% -> 28.4% (the user: fine with labels changing every season); max share random league 4.5% (Glue Wing), real 2016-2025 4.3% (Two-Way Contract); position conflicts 0. **Ratings Regenerator aims at the bars** (`v2/aim.ts`): with the bars alone it reached an aimed build for 19 of 160 test players (it aimed at the build's shape, so a 38-55 ovr player aimed at a high-bar build got an honest name). Now (1) a build is offered only from the ovr where its name can be had (`aimMinOvr`: an elite name 60, a name with fewer than two gated skill words 50): the class archetype pool, the per-player target picker (Draft Scouting's Regenerate ratings, at the ovr he will be built to) and Customize Player's Regenerate build list them by it (`getArchetypeTargets` `minOvr`, `eligibleArchetypes`); (2) a missed aim pushes each rating the target's words fall short of up to its gate plus 2 (`gatePush`; under ovr 50 up to the full skill bar, since such a label needs two skills over it), on top of the shape correction, in up to 10 attempts (was 4; only a miss pays them); (3) the preview's labels are read with his ovr and potential, as the game reads him (`fuzzedArchetypeLabel` level). Round trip (`archetypes.test.ts`, 160 players of ovr 38+ aimed at builds they can have at their ovr): 80 of 160 reach their target (before the bars 92 of 160, at any ovr). Tests: `v2/grammar.test.ts` (every honest name composes and reads back; elite words only from 60; depth and developing names; a specialist; a whole weak league with no elite words and every word true), `worker/core/player/archetype.test.ts` (an honest name's why line), `archetypeFeedback.test.ts`.
- **archnouns4 (the user's 2053 league audit: every word keeps its promise, trait-gated words, fewer High Flyers; reading 26):** the user audited 180 labels of a 2053 league (16 primary labels OK) and suggested names for 70 more. Causes: names from the previous engine (builds such as Wing-Running High Flyer, Rim-Running Hammer, Physical Defensive Rebounder, Open-Lane Burner) and the user's own special names were picked on their nouns only, while their other words promised more (Wing-Running: motor; Hammer: strength; Defensive: perimeter D; Step Back: handle and shot creation; Pest: hustle; Switch: defensive versatility); the defensive label ignored traits; Jack-Of-All-Trades took 5 skills in the top 40%; leaping cleared like any skill. **Fix** (`v2/nouns.ts`): `WORD_REQ` / `namePromises` read every word of every name (special names, the user's names, noun names, end words, honest names, defensive labels): a skill word needs that skill in the league's top 30% and among his three best, Gunner / Shot Hunter a 3PT or mid-range skill, Scorer a scoring skill, Slasher / Open-Lane a handle in the top 50% and speed, Step Back / Shot Creator the shot-creation sub-rating, Two-Way a defensive and an offensive skill, trait words their trait (motor 50, hustle 55, defensive versatility 55, consistency 55, shot discipline 50, clutch / off-ball / veteran savvy 55, screen 50), elite words an elite ovr (league top 10%, 55+; was top 15%), development words ovr 30 or less or no skill; a name that breaks a promise gives way to the next (an honest name to a synonym, another of his skills, then a depth name: `engine.ts`). Jack-Of-All-Trades needs 4 skills in the top 30%. The defensive label's Pest needs hustle 55 and Switch Defender defensive versatility 55 (else the perimeter, steals or rim label); a user special name about offense keeps his defensive label. High Flyers: leaping clears from the top 10% and ranks `ATH_DISCOUNT` 0.04 below a skill at the same standing, and a High-Flying name needs finishing in the top 40% (or leaping in the top 5%). The Ratings Regenerator no longer aims at a name whose words promise what it can't set (`aimReachable`). **Scorecards:** `v2/auditL1.script.test.ts` (`RUN_AUDIT_L1=<dir>`: the 2053 files, league standings rebuilt from the standings the audit quotes): main labels keeping every promise 50% -> 88%, defensive labels 49% -> 97%, High-Flying names 12% -> 5%; the 2048 audit (`nounsAudit.script.test.ts`) 94% -> 92% (the difference: top-3% leapers who no longer get High-Flying without a finisher's touch, and defensive labels now kept with a special name). Fresh new-defaults league, 3 seasons (`worker/core/player/archetypeDiag.script.test.ts`, `RUN_ARCH_DIAG=<dir>`; also dumps every rostered player's ratings, stats, label, nouns and why): High-Flying / High Flyer 13.2% -> 4.6% of labels (real 2026 players 11.9% -> 4.0%), Jack 6.5% -> 3.9%, defensive labels 19.8% -> 27.1%, distinct labels 435 -> 496, top 10 labels 21.9% -> 16.3%. Not changed: labels still change season to season for 48% of player-seasons (only last season's noun pair is sticky), and labels read ratings only (`useStats` false), so a label can disagree with the box score. `v2/archNames.script.test.ts` (`RUN_ARCH_NAMES=<csv>`) writes every name with its engine id, kind, gate and promises.
- **archstick (sticky labels: last season's whole label holds while it still passes every rule; no new reading):** labels changed season to season for 48% of player-seasons, because only last season's noun pair was sticky. **Fix** (`v2/nouns.ts pickNouns`, `engine.ts`, `worker/core/player/archetype.ts storeArchetype`): last season's whole label (main name with its end word, trait words and word variants; a special name, the user's own name, Jack-Of-All-Trades or a trait name; and the defensive label, now passed in as `previousDefense`) is kept while every rule that could give it still holds now (each word's promise, `WORD_REQ` / `namePromises`; the gates and tests of its kind of name: special names' three-best / best-noun / size / elite rules, the user's names' tests, end words' tests, Jack's, the one-noun and development rules; the user's verdicts; the defensive label's own tests, with its skill among his three best as before) and the nouns it names stand within `STICK_MARGIN` 0.05 (mean league standing) of this season's pick; a one-noun name is kept over a two-noun pick only while the extra noun is within the margin of its bar. A defensive label that just appeared (each defensive noun within the margin of the cut, not his best skill) is not added to a kept name. Development and honest names are never kept over a new kind of name: the engine keeps an honest name's words only for the same honest kind. Tie-breaks: nouns at the same standing sort by a fixed order, and a near tie (`STICK_TIE` = `HYST` 0.02) goes to the nouns last season's label named, the same way for the pick and for the words' promises (used only when it keeps more of last season's label than the plain order, so it never gives a third label). Stored entries stay valid (reading 26). **Results** (fresh new-defaults league, 4 seasons, `archetypeDiag.script.test.ts`): label changed 47.7% -> 29.6% of player-seasons (seed 2: 50.8% -> 31.8%); main name 43.5% -> 26.7%, defensive label 13.6% -> 7.9%; with a mean rating change under 1 point 33% -> 16%, 5+ points 88% -> 57%. Changes between two noun names fell by half (337 -> 153); what remains is mostly a rule that no longer holds (a word's noun out of his three best or the top 30%, an end word's, special name's or user name's test, Jack's) and honest names (ovr crossing 50 with one skill, outgrowing a development name), which are not kept by design. Audits unchanged: 2053 main labels 88%, defensive 97%; 2048 92%.
- **Stored labels and stats:** labels are stored at the preseason from the previous regular season's stats; a live reading uses the same season's stats, so the two agree. Stickiness (`STICKY_Z` 2.4): a stored label is kept unless a new build beats it by that much and he still lives up to its name.
- **Scouting fuzz:** BBGM's fuzz is one offset on every rating but height, so it barely changes the shape of a game; a player's label is read from his true ratings (fuzz removed), for every team, so it does not flicker when a new season's fuzz is drawn. It shows no number, so it gives away little: at most, through the absolute part of the reading, a hint of which way his fuzz goes. Draft prospects are the exception: scouting them is part of the game and their fuzz is large, so they are read from what the user's scouts see.
- **Level-invariant matching (arch9; arch10 blends in position-relative standing and stats, see How a player is read):** every skill dimension is read for his size and relative to HIS OWN level (`zVector` in `norms.ts` removes the mean of his core skills in full, and the league-wide `abs` reading is relative too), so a 45 and an 80 whose ratings have the same shape get the same build. Name floors (`need`/`needFor`) are on that relative scale: a "Sharpshooter" needs the three to stand out for him, whatever his overall.
- **Generator:** `twoK/build2k.script.test.ts` (`RUN_ARCHETYPE_2K=1`) converts each 2K build to BBGM ratings with percentile translation tables (per 2K position against generated players; inches to hgt from real players), reads each converted build the way a player is read, takes each name's profile as the centroid of its builds' shapes plus what the name promises (`twoK/concepts.ts`: "2-Way" = defense at least at his own level, "Sharpshooter" = the three, "Glass/Board/Cleaner" = rebounding, "Slasher/Finisher/Dunker" = finishing, ...), positions as the union of its builds' 2K positions (PG -> PG/G, SG -> G/SG/GF, SF -> GF/SF/F, PF -> F/PF/FC, C -> FC/C, narrowed by what the name says), then merges names that say the same thing and, round by round, folds names the matcher (almost) never gives into their nearest neighbour. Ids of old archetypes that are 2K names are kept (`tools/archetypes2k/old-ids.json`); stored entries for removed ids are read live and replaced at the next preseason, an old hybrid partner is ignored.
- **Finishing dimension:** `fin` (layups and dunks, without the height BBGM's at-the-rim composite is mostly made of), which 2K's Slasher/Finisher/Dunker/Lob names are about.
- **Poor fit:** `isPoorFit` (`classify.ts`): similarity of shape below `POOR_FIT_SCORE` 0.5, or the name's floors missed by more than 0.25 z in all.
